## Details (failures first)

### [PASSED] `src/tests/agents/test_multi_turn.py::test_m1_price_refinement`  (0.0545s)

_M1: User refines price constraint in second turn._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_m1_price_refinement(corpus, cf_setup):
    """M1: User refines price constraint in second turn."""
    from package.tools import BaselineScorer
    from package.agents import build_tools

    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    knn = ItemKNNRecommender(5).fit(interactions)
    tools = build_tools(knn, BaselineScorer(knn), user2id, item2id, interactions, corpus)
    planner_tools = tools.for_planner_memory()

    session = Session()
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 5}})),
        planner_tools,
        session=session
    )

    # Turn 1: Initial request for games
    results1 = executor.run("gợi ý game")
    assert results1[0]["ok"]
    assert len(session.turns) == 1

    # Update session constraint for price
    session.update_constraints({"price_max": 50})

    # Turn 2: User asks for cheaper items
    price_plan = plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"price_max": "$S.constraints.price_max"}, "limit": 5}})
    executor2 = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(price_plan, price_plan),
        planner_tools,
        session=session
    )
    results2 = executor2.run("rẻ hơn, dưới 50$")
    assert results2[0]["ok"]
    assert len(session.turns) == 2
    # Verify constraint was used
    assert session.constraints["price_max"] == 50
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/agents/test_multi_turn.py::test_m2_position_reference`  (0.0228s)

_M2: User references "the second one" from previous turn._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_m2_position_reference(corpus, cf_setup):
    """M2: User references "the second one" from previous turn."""
    from package.tools import BaselineScorer
    from package.agents import build_tools

    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    knn = ItemKNNRecommender(5).fit(interactions)
    tools = build_tools(knn, BaselineScorer(knn), user2id, item2id, interactions, corpus)
    planner_tools = tools.for_planner_memory()

    session = Session()
    session.add_shown_items([VG_A, VG_B, VG_C])

    # Turn 2: Ask for items similar to the second shown item
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "SimilarTool", "args": {"items": ["$S.shown_items.1"], "top_k": 5}})),
        planner_tools,
        session=session
    )

    results = executor.run("tương tự cái thứ hai")
    assert results[0]["ok"]
    # Verify the reference was resolved to VG_B
    assert VG_B in results[0]["args"]["items"]
```

```python
def test_m3_exclusion_persistence(corpus):
    """M3: User excludes an item/category, and it persists across turns."""
    session = Session()
    session.add_shown_items([VG_A, VG_B])

    # User excludes VG_A
    session.add_exclusions(item_ids=[VG_A])

    # Turn 2: Request should respect the exclusion
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"exclude_item_ids": "$S.constraints.exclude_item_ids"}, "limit": 5}})),
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=session
    )

    results = executor.run("cho thêm vài cái")
    assert results[0]["ok"]
    # Verify exclusion was used
    assert VG_A in session.constraints["exclude_item_ids"]
```

```python
def test_m4_no_repeat_of_shown_items(corpus):
    """M4: Show more items should not repeat already shown items."""
    session = Session()
    session.add_shown_items([VG_A, VG_B])

    # Turn 2: Request more items
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"exclude_item_ids": "$S.shown_items"}, "limit": 5}})),
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=session
    )

    results = executor.run("cho thêm vài cái nữa")
    assert results[0]["ok"]
    # Verify shown items were used for exclusion
    assert session.shown_items == [VG_A, VG_B]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/agents/test_multi_turn.py::test_m5_clarification_flow`  (0.0093s)

_M5: Agent asks for clarification, user provides it._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_m5_clarification_flow(corpus):
    """M5: Agent asks for clarification, user provides it."""
    session = Session()

    # Turn 1: Agent asks for clarification (ask_user)
    ask_user_plan = json.dumps({"steps": [], "ask_user": "What product type do you like?"})
    executor1 = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(ask_user_plan),
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=session
    )

    results1 = executor1.run("suggest something")
    assert results1 == []  # No steps when ask_user
    assert executor1.last_question == "What product type do you like?"
    assert len(session.turns) == 1

    # Turn 2: User provides clarification
    clarification_plan = plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"domain": "Video_Games"}, "limit": 5}})
    executor2 = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(clarification_plan, clarification_plan, clarification_plan),  # Provide enough responses
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=session
    )

    results2 = executor2.run("I like games")
    if not results2[0]["ok"]:
        print(f"Error: {results2[0].get('error')}")
    assert results2[0]["ok"]
    assert len(session.turns) == 2
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_m6_topic_change(corpus):
    """M6: User changes topic, old constraints should be cleared or not used."""
    session = Session()
    session.update_constraints({"price_max": 50, "category": "Video_Games"})

    # Turn 2: User changes topic to electronics
    session.reset()  # Or clear specific constraints
    session.update_constraints({"category": "Electronics"})

    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"categories_any": ["Electronics"]}, "limit": 5}})),
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=session
    )

    results = executor.run("đổi sang đồ điện tử")
    assert results[0]["ok"]
    # Verify new constraint is used
    assert session.constraints["category"] == "Electronics"
```

_M7: User asks for recommendations based on long-term preferences._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_m7_long_term_memory_usage(corpus, cf_setup):
    """M7: User asks for recommendations based on long-term preferences."""
    from package.tools import BaselineScorer
    from package.agents import build_tools

    interactions, user2id, item2id = cf_setup
    knn = ItemKNNRecommender(5).fit(interactions)
    tools = build_tools(knn, BaselineScorer(knn), user2id, item2id, interactions, corpus)
    planner_tools = tools.for_planner_memory()

    session = Session()

    # Simplified test: just verify session is preserved across turns with memory tools
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 5}})),
        planner_tools,
        session=session
    )

    results = executor.run("gợi ý theo sở thích của tôi")
    assert results[0]["ok"]
    # Verify session state is updated
    assert len(session.turns) == 1
```

```python
def test_session_limits_turn_truncation():
    """Test that old turns are removed when max_turns is exceeded."""
    session = Session(max_turns=3)

    for i in range(5):
        session.add_turn(f"request {i}", f"plan {i}", f"answer {i}")

    assert len(session.turns) == 3
    assert session.turns[0].user_request == "request 2"
```

```python
def test_multi_turn_with_feedback(corpus):
    """Test that user feedback is recorded in session."""
    session = Session()
    session.add_shown_items([VG_A, VG_B])

    # User likes VG_A, dislikes VG_B
    session.add_feedback(VG_A, "like")
    session.add_feedback(VG_B, "dislike")

    assert session.feedback[VG_A] == "like"
    assert session.feedback[VG_B] == "dislike"
```

```python
def test_session_reset_clears_all_state():
    """Test that session.reset() clears all state."""
    session = Session()
    session.add_turn("request", "plan", "answer")
    session.add_shown_items([VG_A])
    session.set_candidates([VG_B], [VG_C])
    session.update_constraints({"price_max": 100})
    session.add_feedback(VG_A, "like")

    session.reset()

    assert session.turns == []
    assert session.shown_items == []
    assert session.candidates == []
    assert session.ranked == []
    assert session.constraints == {}
    assert session.feedback == {}
```

```python
def test_two_independent_sessions_do_not_interfere():

    session1 = Session()
    session2 = Session()

    session1.add_shown_items([VG_A])
    session2.add_shown_items([VG_B])

    assert session1.shown_items == [VG_A]
    assert session2.shown_items == [VG_B]

    assert session1.get_valid_ids() == {VG_A}
    assert session2.get_valid_ids() == {VG_B}
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/agents/test_multi_turn.py::test_session_none_means_no_session_state`  (0.0083s)

_Test that session=None means no session state is created or injected._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_session_none_means_no_session_state(corpus):
    """Test that session=None means no session state is created or injected."""
    # Create executor without session
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 5}})),
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=None
    )

    # Verify no session is created
    assert executor.session is None

    # Run request
    results = executor.run("gợi ý game")
    assert results[0]["ok"]

    # Still no session
    assert executor.session is None
```

```python
def test_no_session_leak_between_requests(corpus):
    """Test that requests on same executor with session=None are independent."""
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 5}}), plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 5}})),
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=None
    )

    # Request 1 with item IDs in text
    results1 = executor.run(f"show me {VG_A}")
    assert results1[0]["ok"]

    # Request 2 should not see VG_A from request 1
    # If session leaked, VG_A would be in valid_ids
    results2 = executor.run("show something else")
    assert results2[0]["ok"]

    # Verify no session was created
    assert executor.session is None
```

```python
def test_explicit_session_preserves_state(corpus):
    """Test that explicit session preserves state across turns."""
    session = Session()
    executor = PlanExecutor(
        ScriptedLLM(plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 5}}), plan({"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"limit": 5}})),
        [FilterTool(corpus)],
        session=session
    )

    # Turn 1
    results1 = executor.run("gợi ý game")
    assert results1[0]["ok"]
    assert len(session.turns) == 1

    # Turn 2 on same executor with same session
    results2 = executor.run("cho thêm")
    assert results2[0]["ok"]
    assert len(session.turns) == 2

    # Session is preserved
    assert executor.session is session
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_dataset_and_evaluator_fixes.py::test_iso_leak_shared_session_scores_zero`  (0.0289s)

_Experimental proof: if iso_2 runs on a session that still carries_

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_iso_leak_shared_session_scores_zero(conversations):
    """Experimental proof: if iso_2 runs on a session that still carries
    iso_1's exclusion, a plan that propagates it MUST score 0 via
    absent_exclusions. Control: same session, clean plan -> 1.0."""
    from dataclasses import asdict

    from package.agents import get_session_limits
    from package.agents.session import Session

    conv = next(c for c in conversations if c.conversation_id == "conv_session_isolation_2")

    def leaked_session() -> Session:
        s = Session(**asdict(get_session_limits()))
        s.add_shown_items(["Video_Games::vg_b", "Video_Games::vg_e"])
        s.add_exclusions(item_ids=["Video_Games::vg_a"])  # what iso_1 leaves behind
        return s

    leaked_plan = json.dumps({
        "steps": [{"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {
            "filters": {"categories_any": ["Puzzle"], "exclude_item_ids": ["Video_Games::vg_a"]},
            "limit": 10}}],
        "ask_user": None,
    })
    clean_plan = json.dumps({
        "steps": [{"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {
            "filters": {"categories_any": ["Puzzle"]}, "limit": 10}}],
        "ask_user": None,
    })

    runner_leak, _ = _runner_with_script(leaked_plan)
    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    evaluator._initialize_session = lambda ctx: leaked_session()
    res = evaluator.evaluate_conversation(conv, runner_leak)
    assert res.turn_results[0].metrics["exclusion_compliance"] == 0.0

    runner_clean, _ = _runner_with_script(clean_plan)
    evaluator2 = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    evaluator2._initialize_session = lambda ctx: leaked_session()
    res2 = evaluator2.evaluate_conversation(conv, runner_clean)
    assert res2.turn_results[0].metrics["exclusion_compliance"] == 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_dataset_and_evaluator_fixes.py::test_real_agent_runner_smoke_two_turns`  (0.0139s)

_Scripted plans through the REAL executor+tools must not crash._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_agent_runner_smoke_two_turns(conversations):
    """Scripted plans through the REAL executor+tools must not crash."""
    conv = next(c for c in conversations if c.conversation_id == "conv_m1_price_refinement")
    plan1 = json.dumps({"steps": [{"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"domain": "Video_Games"}, "limit": 5}}], "ask_user": None})
    plan2 = json.dumps({"steps": [{"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"price_max": 50, "domain": "Video_Games"}, "limit": 5}}], "ask_user": None})
    runner, llm = _runner_with_script(plan1, plan2)
    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    result = evaluator.evaluate_conversation(conv, runner)
    assert result.conversation_success
    assert len(result.turn_results) == 2
    assert all(tr.success for tr in result.turn_results)
    # t2 must show the price constraint reached the executed filter args
    assert result.turn_results[1].metrics["exclusion_compliance"] == 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_dataset_and_evaluator_fixes.py::test_real_agent_runner_smoke_m2_session_reference`  (0.0152s)

_m2: $S.shown_items.1 must resolve and reach SimilarTool as the seed._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_agent_runner_smoke_m2_session_reference(conversations):
    """m2: $S.shown_items.1 must resolve and reach SimilarTool as the seed."""
    conv = next(c for c in conversations if c.conversation_id == "conv_m2_position_reference")
    # t1: FilterTool -> LookupTool($1.candidates.*.item_id) populates
    # session.shown_items deterministically (LookupTool alone would trip
    # the invented-id guard: bare ids are not in the request text).
    plan1 = json.dumps({"steps": [
        {"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"domain": "Video_Games"}, "limit": 3}},
        {"tool": "LookupTool", "args": {"item_ids": "$1.candidates.*.item_id"}},
    ], "ask_user": None})
    # t2: the plan itself uses the session reference the dataset asserts
    plan2 = json.dumps({"steps": [{"tool": "SimilarTool", "args": {
        "items": ["$S.shown_items.1"], "top_k": 5}}], "ask_user": None})
    runner, _ = _runner_with_script(plan1, plan2)
    result = MultiTurnEvaluator().evaluate_conversation(conv, runner)
    assert result.conversation_success
    t2 = result.turn_results[1]
    assert t2.success
    # expected: SimilarTool seeded with the 2nd shown item (vg_b)
    assert t2.metrics["tool_call_match_rate"] == 1.0
    step = next(s for s in t2.trace["trace"]["steps"] if s["tool"] == "SimilarTool")
    assert step["ok"], step.get("error")
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_dataset_and_evaluator_fixes.py::test_real_agent_runner_smoke_m7_memory_then_similar`  (0.0262s)

_m7: MemoryTool(u_long) then SimilarTool over its recent_items._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_agent_runner_smoke_m7_memory_then_similar(conversations):
    """m7: MemoryTool(u_long) then SimilarTool over its recent_items."""
    conv = next(c for c in conversations if c.conversation_id == "conv_m7_long_term_memory")
    plan1 = json.dumps({"steps": [{"tool": "MemoryTool", "args": {"user_id": "u_long"}}],
                        "ask_user": None})
    # t2: MemoryTool output is NOT persisted across turns, so the real
    # planner re-fetches memory and chains SimilarTool in the SAME plan
    # ($1 = this turn's step 1, not the previous turn).
    plan2 = json.dumps({"steps": [
        {"tool": "MemoryTool", "args": {"user_id": "u_long"}},
        {"tool": "SimilarTool", "args": {"items": "$1.recent_items", "top_k": 5}},
    ], "ask_user": None})
    runner, _ = _runner_with_script(plan1, plan2)
    result = MultiTurnEvaluator().evaluate_conversation(conv, runner)
    assert result.conversation_success
    assert result.turn_results[0].metrics["tool_call_match_rate"] == 1.0  # MemoryTool u_long
    t2 = result.turn_results[1]
    assert t2.metrics["tool_call_match_rate"] == 1.0  # SimilarTool called
    step = next(s for s in t2.trace["trace"]["steps"] if s["tool"] == "SimilarTool")
    assert step["ok"], step.get("error")
```

```python
def test_enabled_with_output_dir_redirects(self, tmp_path):
    target = tmp_path / "campaign" / "multi_turn"
    info = _eval_step.run_multi_turn_for_eval(
        {"enabled": True, "output_dir": str(target)}, tmp_path / "exp_001" / "multi_turn.json"
    )
    assert info["output_path"] == str((target / "multi_turn.json").resolve())
    # The experiment dir must NOT receive a copy — single location only.
    assert not (tmp_path / "exp_001" / "multi_turn.json").exists()
    payload = json.loads((target / "multi_turn.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert payload["status"] == "skipped_no_credentials"
    assert "reason" in payload
```

```python
def test_success_path_parses_summary(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    out = tmp_path / "mt.json"

    def fake_run(cmd, **kwargs):
        output = Path(cmd[cmd.index("--output") + 1])
        output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output.write_text(json.dumps(_summary_payload()), encoding="utf-8")
        return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="ok", stderr="")

    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step.subprocess, "run", fake_run)
    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step, "_llm_configured", lambda: True)

    info = _eval_step.run_multi_turn_eval(dataset="d.jsonl", output_path=out)
    assert info["status"] == "success"
    assert info["resumed"] is False
    assert info["summary"]["turn_success_rate"] == 0.9
```

```python
def test_resume_returns_existing_success_without_subprocess(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):

    out.write_text(json.dumps(_summary_payload()), encoding="utf-8")

    # when credentials are absent.
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_eval_step.py::TestRunMultiTurnEval::test_non_success_statuses_rerun_when_llm_available`  (0.0277s)

_skipped_no_credentials / error / failed files must NOT count as done:_

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_non_success_statuses_rerun_when_llm_available(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """skipped_no_credentials / error / failed files must NOT count as done:
    a later run with credentials has to execute for real."""
    calls = []

    def fake_run(cmd, **kwargs):
        calls.append(cmd)
        output = Path(cmd[cmd.index("--output") + 1])
        output.write_text(json.dumps(_summary_payload()), encoding="utf-8")
        return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="ok", stderr="")

    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step.subprocess, "run", fake_run)
    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step, "_llm_configured", lambda: True)

    for stale_status in ("skipped_no_credentials", "error", "failed", "timeout"):
        out = tmp_path / f"mt_{stale_status}.json"
        out.write_text(json.dumps({"status": stale_status}), encoding="utf-8")
        info = _eval_step.run_multi_turn_eval(dataset="d.jsonl", output_path=out)
        assert info["status"] == "success"
        assert info["resumed"] is False
    assert len(calls) == 4
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_timeout_writes_timeout_status(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    out = tmp_path / "mt.json"

    def fake_run(cmd, **kwargs):
        raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=cmd, timeout=kwargs.get("timeout"))

    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step.subprocess, "run", fake_run)
    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step, "_llm_configured", lambda: True)

    info = _eval_step.run_multi_turn_eval(
        dataset="d.jsonl", output_path=out, timeout_seconds=5
    )
    assert info["status"] == "timeout"
    assert "timed out" in info["error"]
    payload = json.loads(out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert payload["status"] == "timeout"
```

```python
        output = Path(cmd[cmd.index("--output") + 1])

        {"enabled": True, "timeout_seconds": 42}, tmp_path / "exp" / "multi_turn.json"
    )
    assert seen["timeout"] == 42
```

```python
def test_relative_output_dir_resolves_under_experiments(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    fake_paths = SimpleNamespace(experiments_dir=tmp_path / "experiments", src_root=tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step, "get_data_paths", lambda: fake_paths)
    info = _eval_step.run_multi_turn_eval(
        dataset="d.jsonl", output_path="agent_eval/campaigns/c1/multi_turn/multi_turn.json"
    )
    expected = tmp_path / "experiments" / "agent_eval" / "campaigns" / "c1" / "multi_turn" / "multi_turn.json"
    assert info["output_path"] == str(expected.resolve())
    assert expected.exists()
```

```python
def test_conversation_serialization():
    """Test Conversation serialization round-trip."""
    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_001",
        user_id="user_001",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Turn 1",
                expected_behavior="Behavior 1",
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="Turn 2",
                expected_behavior="Behavior 2",
            ),
        ],
        initial_context={"key": "value"},
        expected_outcome="Success",
        tags=["multi_turn"],
        metadata={"scenario": "test"},
    )

    data = conversation.to_dict()
    restored = Conversation.from_dict(data)

    assert restored.conversation_id == conversation.conversation_id
    assert len(restored.turns) == 2
    assert restored.initial_context == conversation.initial_context
```

```python
def test_conversation_result_serialization():
    """Test ConversationResult serialization round-trip."""
    result = ConversationResult(
        conversation_id="conv_001",
        turn_results=[
            TurnResult(
                turn_id="t1",
                actual_items=["item1"],
                success=True,
                metrics={"item_match_rate": 1.0},
            ),
        ],
        conversation_success=True,
        metrics={"turn_success_rate": 1.0},
    )

    data = result.to_dict()
    restored = ConversationResult.from_dict(data)

    assert restored.conversation_id == result.conversation_id
    assert len(restored.turn_results) == 1
    assert restored.conversation_success == result.conversation_success
```

```python
def test_multi_turn_evaluator_conversation_metrics():
    """Test conversation-level metrics calculation."""
    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()

    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_001",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Turn 1",
                expected_items=["item1"],
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="Turn 2",
                expected_items=["item2"],
            ),
        ],
    )

    # Mock turn results
    turn_results = [
        TurnResult(
            turn_id="t1",
            actual_items=["item1"],
            success=True,
            metrics={"item_match_rate": 1.0, "exclusion_compliance": 1.0},
            session_state={"shown_items": ["item1"], "constraints": {}},
        ),
        TurnResult(
            turn_id="t2",
            actual_items=["item2"],
            success=True,
            metrics={"item_match_rate": 1.0, "exclusion_compliance": 1.0},
            session_state={"shown_items": ["item1", "item2"], "constraints": {}},
        ),
    ]

    metrics = evaluator._calculate_conversation_metrics(turn_results, conversation)

    assert metrics["turn_success_rate"] == 1.0
    assert metrics["conversation_success"] == 1.0
    assert metrics["avg_item_match_rate"] == 1.0
    assert metrics["context_retention"] == 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_evaluator.py::test_session_isolation`  (0.0007s)

_Test that two independent conversations do not share session state._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_session_isolation():
    """Test that two independent conversations do not share session state."""
    # This is a unit test for the concept; actual session isolation is tested in test_session.py
    session1 = {"turns": [], "shown_items": [], "constraints": {}}
    session2 = {"turns": [], "shown_items": [], "constraints": {}}

    session1["shown_items"] = ["item1"]
    session2["shown_items"] = ["item2"]

    assert session1["shown_items"] == ["item1"]
    assert session2["shown_items"] == ["item2"]
    assert session1["shown_items"] != session2["shown_items"]
```

```python
def test_context_retention_detection():
    """Test that context retention is detected correctly."""
    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()

    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_001",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Turn 1",
                expected_items=["item1"],
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="Turn 2",
                expected_items=["item2"],
            ),
        ],
    )

    # Test monotonic growth (should pass)
    turn_results_monotonic = [
        TurnResult(
            turn_id="t1",
            actual_items=["item1"],
            success=True,
            metrics={},
            session_state={"shown_items": ["item1"], "constraints": {}},
        ),
        TurnResult(
            turn_id="t2",
            actual_items=["item1", "item2"],
            success=True,
            metrics={},
            session_state={"shown_items": ["item1", "item2"], "constraints": {}},
        ),
    ]

    metrics = evaluator._calculate_conversation_metrics(turn_results_monotonic, conversation)
    assert metrics["context_retention"] == 1.0

    # Test non-monotonic (should fail)
    turn_results_non_monotonic = [
        TurnResult(
            turn_id="t1",
            actual_items=["item1", "item2"],
            success=True,
            metrics={},
            session_state={"shown_items": ["item1", "item2"], "constraints": {}},
        ),
        TurnResult(
            turn_id="t2",
            actual_items=["item1"],
            success=True,
            metrics={},
            session_state={"shown_items": ["item1"], "constraints": {}},
        ),
    ]

    metrics = evaluator._calculate_conversation_metrics(turn_results_non_monotonic, conversation)
    assert metrics["context_retention"] == 0.0
```

```python
def test_turn_input_expect_flags_roundtrip():
    t = TurnInput(
        turn_id="t", position=0, user_request="r",
        expect_clarification=True, expect_no_result=True,
    )
    restored = TurnInput.from_dict(t.to_dict())
    assert restored.expect_clarification is True
    assert restored.expect_no_result is True
    # absent keys default to False (backward compat with old datasets)
    old = TurnInput.from_dict({"turn_id": "x", "position": 0, "user_request": "r"})
    assert old.expect_clarification is False and old.expect_no_result is False
```

```python
def test_conversation_metrics_include_compliance_means():
    ev = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    conv = Conversation(conversation_id="c", turns=[
        TurnInput(turn_id="t1", position=0, user_request="r", expect_no_result=True),
        TurnInput(turn_id="t2", position=1, user_request="r"),
    ])
    results = [
        # t1: empty result as expected; t2: no expectation -> excluded from mean
        SimpleNamespace(metrics={"no_result_compliance": 1.0, "clarification_compliance": None}),
        SimpleNamespace(metrics={"no_result_compliance": None, "clarification_compliance": None}),
    ]
    # _calculate_conversation_metrics reads tr.metrics + tr.success via TurnResult;
    # use real TurnResult objects for safety
    from package.evaluation.multi_turn import TurnResult
    results = [
        TurnResult(turn_id="t1", success=True, metrics={"no_result_compliance": 1.0}),
        TurnResult(turn_id="t2", success=True, metrics={"no_result_compliance": None}),
    ]
    m = ev._calculate_conversation_metrics(results, conv)
    assert m["no_result_compliance"] == 1.0  # mean over the ONE expecting turn
    assert "clarification_compliance" not in m
```

```python
def test_run_multi_turn_eval_forwards_limits_json(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    out = tmp_path / "mt.json"
    seen = {}

    def fake_run(cmd, **kwargs):
        seen["cmd"] = cmd
        Path(cmd[cmd.index("--output") + 1]).write_text(
            json.dumps({"turn_success_rate": 1.0}), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="", stderr="")

    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step.subprocess, "run", fake_run)
    monkeypatch.setattr(_eval_step, "_llm_configured", lambda: True)
    _eval_step.run_multi_turn_eval(
        dataset="d.jsonl", output_path=out, limits={"max_steps_per_turn": 4}
    )
    assert "--limits-json" in seen["cmd"]
    payload = json.loads(seen["cmd"][seen["cmd"].index("--limits-json") + 1])
    assert payload == {"max_steps_per_turn": 4}
```

```python
    assert out["avg_item_match_rate_mean"] == 0.72
    assert out["exclusion_compliance_mean"] == 0.95
    assert out["clarification_compliance_mean"] == 0.5
    assert "unrelated_mean" not in out
```

```python
def test_turn_counts_fail_on_first_turn():
    # conv_m1_t1 fails -> conv_m1 stops after 1 executed turn; its t2 never runs.
    s = _summary(FailOnTurn("m1_t1"))
    assert s.total_turns_scheduled == 24

    assert s.turn_completion_ratio == pytest.approx(23 / 24)

    assert s.failed_conversations == 1

    assert s.conversation_success_rate == pytest.approx(12 / 13)
```

```python
def test_from_dict_old_json_without_new_fields():

        "total_conversations": 11,
        "turn_success_rate": 0.9,
```

```python
def test_run_all_multi_turn_old_result_file_failed_cases_none(tmp_path):
    # Result files written before the metric fixes have no failed_conversations.
    f = _write_mt_result(tmp_path / "mt.json")
    data = json.loads(Path(f).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for k in ("failed_conversations", "failed_conversation_ids",
              "turn_completion_ratio", "total_turns_scheduled",
              "total_turns_executed"):
        data.pop(k, None)
    Path(f).write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
    agg = _run_all.aggregate_results({"multi_turn": _info(f)})
    mt = agg["components"]["multi_turn"]
    assert mt["failed_cases"] is None  # unknown, not a fake 0
    assert mt["latency"]["p95"] is None
```

```python
def test_session_context_retention():
    """Test that session retains context across turns."""
    session = Session()

    # Turn 1
    session.add_shown_items(["item1", "item2"])
    session.update_constraints({"price_max": 50})

    assert len(session.shown_items) == 2
    assert session.constraints["price_max"] == 50

    # Turn 2
    session.add_shown_items(["item3"])
    session.update_constraints({"category": "Action"})

    assert len(session.shown_items) == 3
    assert session.constraints["price_max"] == 50
    assert session.constraints["category"] == "Action"
```

```python
def test_session_exclusion_persistence():
    """Test that exclusions persist across turns."""
    session = Session()

    # Turn 1: Add exclusion
    session.add_exclusions(item_ids=["item1"], categories=["Puzzle"])

    assert "item1" in session.constraints["exclude_item_ids"]
    assert "Puzzle" in session.constraints["exclude_categories"]

    # Turn 2: Add more exclusions
    session.add_exclusions(item_ids=["item2"])

    assert "item1" in session.constraints["exclude_item_ids"]
    assert "item2" in session.constraints["exclude_item_ids"]
    assert "Puzzle" in session.constraints["exclude_categories"]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_with_session.py::test_conversation_with_real_session`  (0.0014s)

_Test that the evaluator works with real Session objects._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_conversation_with_real_session():
    """Test that the evaluator works with real Session objects."""
    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_test",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Show me games",
                expected_items=["item1", "item2"],
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="Show me more",
                expected_items=["item3", "item4"],
            ),
        ],
    )

    # Mock agent runner that uses real Session
    class SessionAwareAgentRunner:
        def __init__(self):
            pass

        def run_turn(self, turn_input, session):
            # Simulate agent behavior
            if turn_input.position == 0:
                session.add_shown_items(["item1", "item2"])
                return {"items": ["item1", "item2"], "exclusions": {}}
            elif turn_input.position == 1:
                session.add_shown_items(["item3", "item4"])
                return {"items": ["item3", "item4"], "exclusions": {}}

    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    # Override session initialization to use real Session
    original_init = evaluator._initialize_session
    evaluator._initialize_session = lambda ctx: Session()

    agent_runner = SessionAwareAgentRunner()
    result = evaluator.evaluate_conversation(conversation, agent_runner)

    assert result.conversation_success
    assert len(result.turn_results) == 2
    assert result.turn_results[0].success
    assert result.turn_results[1].success
    assert result.metrics["context_retention"] == 1.0
```

```python
def test_multi_turn_m1_price_refinement_scenario():
    """Test M1: Price refinement scenario with real session."""
    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_m1",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="m1_t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Show me video games",
                expected_items=["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"],
                metadata={"scenario": "M1"},
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="m1_t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="Show me cheaper ones under $50",
                expected_items=["vg_a"],
                expected_exclusions={"price_max": 50},
                metadata={"scenario": "M1"},
            ),
        ],
    )

    class M1AgentRunner:
        def run_turn(self, turn_input, session):
            if turn_input.position == 0:
                session.add_shown_items(["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"])
                return {"items": ["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"], "exclusions": {}}
            elif turn_input.position == 1:
                session.update_constraints({"price_max": 50})
                session.add_shown_items(["vg_a"])
                return {"items": ["vg_a"], "exclusions": {"price_max": 50}}

    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    evaluator._initialize_session = lambda ctx: Session()

    agent_runner = M1AgentRunner()
    result = evaluator.evaluate_conversation(conversation, agent_runner)

    assert result.conversation_success
    assert result.metrics["turn_success_rate"] == 1.0
    assert result.metrics["conversation_success"] == 1.0
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_multi_turn_m3_exclusion_scenario():
    """Test M3: Exclusion persistence scenario."""
    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_m3",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="m3_t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Show me video games",
                expected_items=["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"],
                metadata={"scenario": "M3"},
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="m3_t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="I don't like the first one, show me others",
                expected_items=["vg_d", "vg_e"],
                expected_exclusions={"exclude_item_ids": ["vg_a"]},
                metadata={"scenario": "M3"},
            ),
        ],
    )

    class M3AgentRunner:
        def run_turn(self, turn_input, session):
            if turn_input.position == 0:
                session.add_shown_items(["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"])
                return {"items": ["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"], "exclusions": {}}
            elif turn_input.position == 1:
                session.add_exclusions(item_ids=["vg_a"])
                session.add_shown_items(["vg_d", "vg_e"])
                return {"items": ["vg_d", "vg_e"], "exclusions": {"exclude_item_ids": ["vg_a"]}}

    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    evaluator._initialize_session = lambda ctx: Session()

    agent_runner = M3AgentRunner()
    result = evaluator.evaluate_conversation(conversation, agent_runner)

    assert result.conversation_success
    assert result.metrics["exclusion_compliance"] == 1.0
```

```python
def test_multi_turn_m4_more_items_scenario():
    """Test M4: More items without repetition."""
    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_m4",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="m4_t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Show me video games",
                expected_items=["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"],
                metadata={"scenario": "M4"},
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="m4_t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="Show me more",
                expected_items=["vg_d", "vg_e", "vg_f"],
                expected_exclusions={"exclude_item_ids": ["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"]},
                metadata={"scenario": "M4"},
            ),
        ],
    )

    class M4AgentRunner:
        def run_turn(self, turn_input, session):
            if turn_input.position == 0:
                session.add_shown_items(["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"])
                return {"items": ["vg_a", "vg_b", "vg_c"], "exclusions": {}}
            elif turn_input.position == 1:
                # Exclude shown items
                session.add_exclusions(item_ids=session.shown_items)
                session.add_shown_items(["vg_d", "vg_e", "vg_f"])
                return {"items": ["vg_d", "vg_e", "vg_f"], "exclusions": {"exclude_item_ids": session.shown_items}}

    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    evaluator._initialize_session = lambda ctx: Session()

    agent_runner = M4AgentRunner()
    result = evaluator.evaluate_conversation(conversation, agent_runner)

    assert result.conversation_success
    assert result.metrics["context_retention"] == 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/multi_turn/test_with_session.py::test_conversation_failure_propagates`  (0.0014s)

_Test that a failed turn causes conversation failure._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_conversation_failure_propagates():
    """Test that a failed turn causes conversation failure."""
    conversation = Conversation(
        conversation_id="conv_fail",
        turns=[
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t1",
                position=0,
                user_request="Valid request",
                expected_items=["item1"],
            ),
            TurnInput(
                turn_id="t2",
                position=1,
                user_request="Invalid request",
                expected_items=[],
            ),
        ],
    )

    class FailingAgentRunner:
        def run_turn(self, turn_input, session):
            if turn_input.position == 0:
                session.add_shown_items(["item1"])
                return {"items": ["item1"], "exclusions": {}}
            elif turn_input.position == 1:
                raise ValueError("Simulated error")

    evaluator = MultiTurnEvaluator()
    evaluator._initialize_session = lambda ctx: Session()

    agent_runner = FailingAgentRunner()
    result = evaluator.evaluate_conversation(conversation, agent_runner)

    assert not result.conversation_success
    assert result.failure_stage == "turn_1"
    assert result.error == "Simulated error"
    assert result.turn_results[0].success
    assert not result.turn_results[1].success
```
